  `name` varchar(200) NOT NULL,
  `schedule_day` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `schedule_time` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `coach_name` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `color` varchar(50) DEFAULT '#3b82f6',
  `address` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `phone` varchar(20) DEFAULT NULL,
  `email` varchar(320) DEFAULT NULL,
  `status` enum('active','inactive') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
  `notes` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `updated_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=9 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

--
-- Table structure for table `elite_payments`
--

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `elite_payments`;
/*!40101 SET @saved_cs_client     = @@character_set_client */;
/*!40101 SET character_set_client = utf8mb4 */;
CREATE TABLE `elite_payments` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `student_id` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `class_count` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `amount` decimal(10,2) NOT NULL,
  `receipt_url` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `receipt_key` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `payment_date` timestamp NOT NULL,
  `confirmed_by` enum('parent_upload','admin_approved') DEFAULT 'admin_approved',
  `status` enum('pending','confirmed') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'confirmed',
  `notes` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `updated_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `elite_payments_student_id_elite_students_id_fk` (`student_id`),
  CONSTRAINT `elite_payments_student_id_elite_students_id_fk` FOREIGN KEY (`student_id`) REFERENCES `elite_students` (`id`) ON DELETE NO ACTION ON UPDATE NO ACTION
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=75 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;
/*!40101 SET character_set_client = @saved_cs_client */;

--
-- Table structure for table `elite_schedules`
--

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `elite_schedules`;
/*!40101 SET @saved_cs_client     = @@character_set_client */;
/*!40101 SET character_set_client = utf8mb4 */;
CREATE TABLE `elite_schedules` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `training_date` timestamp NOT NULL,
  `schedule_day` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `schedule_time` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `status` enum('active','cancelled') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
  `notes` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `updated_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=209 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;
/*!40101 SET character_set_client = @saved_cs_client */;

  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,

  `phone` varchar(50) NOT NULL,

  `notes` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `updated_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  `join_date` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`)

--
-- Table structure for table `event_registrations`
--

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `event_registrations`;
/*!40101 SET @saved_cs_client     = @@character_set_client */;
/*!40101 SET character_set_client = utf8mb4 */;
CREATE TABLE `event_registrations` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `event_id` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `student_id` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `elite_student_id` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `student_name` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `phone` varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  `status` enum('registered','confirmed','cancelled') DEFAULT 'registered',
  `notes` text DEFAULT NULL,

--
-- Table structure for table `exam_schedules`
--

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `exam_schedules`;
/*!40101 SET @saved_cs_client     = @@character_set_client */;
/*!40101 SET character_set_client = utf8mb4 */;
CREATE TABLE `exam_schedules` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `exam_id` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `belt_level` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `group_code` varchar(10) DEFAULT NULL,
  `start_time` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `end_time` varchar(30) DEFAULT NULL,
  `time_slot` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `venue` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `notes` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `idx_exam_id` (`exam_id`),
  CONSTRAINT `exam_schedules_ibfk_1` FOREIGN KEY (`exam_id`) REFERENCES `exam_sessions` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=29 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;
/*!40101 SET character_set_client = @saved_cs_client */;

--
-- Table structure for table `exam_scores`
--

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `exam_scores`;
/*!40101 SET @saved_cs_client     = @@character_set_client */;
/*!40101 SET character_set_client = utf8mb4 */;
CREATE TABLE `exam_scores` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `candidate_id` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `scoring_item_id` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `score` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `comment` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `scored_by` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `scored_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `uq_candidate_item` (`candidate_id`,`scoring_item_id`),
  KEY `idx_candidate_id` (`candidate_id`),
  KEY `idx_scoring_item_id` (`scoring_item_id`),
  CONSTRAINT `exam_scores_ibfk_1` FOREIGN KEY (`candidate_id`) REFERENCES `exam_candidates` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `exam_scores_ibfk_2` FOREIGN KEY (`scoring_item_id`) REFERENCES `exam_scoring_items` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=807 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;
/*!40101 SET character_set_client = @saved_cs_client */;

--
-- Table structure for table `exam_scoring_items`
--

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `exam_scoring_items`;
/*!40101 SET @saved_cs_client     = @@character_set_client */;
/*!40101 SET character_set_client = utf8mb4 */;
CREATE TABLE `exam_scoring_items` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `name` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `description` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `type` enum('grade','pass_fail','yes_no') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'grade',
  `category` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `max_score` decimal(5,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 10.00,
  `weight` decimal(3,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1.00,
  `belt_level` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `sort_order` int(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `idx_belt_level` (`belt_level`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=324 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;
/*!40101 SET character_set_client = @saved_cs_client */;

  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `name` varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  `exam_date` date NOT NULL,
  `location` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `description` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `status` enum('draft','scheduled','in_progress','completed') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
  `event_id` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `idx_exam_date` (`exam_date`),
  KEY `idx_status` (`status`),
  KEY `idx_event_id` (`event_id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=3 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

--
-- Table structure for table `payment_reminders`
--

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `payment_reminders`;
/*!40101 SET @saved_cs_client     = @@character_set_client */;
/*!40101 SET character_set_client = utf8mb4 */;
CREATE TABLE `payment_reminders` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `student_id` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `reminded_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `reminded_by` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `month` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `year` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `payment_reminders_student_id_students_id_fk` (`student_id`),
  KEY `payment_reminders_reminded_by_users_id_fk` (`reminded_by`),

CREATE TABLE `students` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `name` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `birthDate` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `phone` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `venue` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `scheduleDay` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `scheduleTime` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `feePerQuarter` decimal(10,2) NOT NULL,
  `beltLevel` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `createdAt` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `updatedAt` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  `student_number` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `gender` enum('male','female','other') DEFAULT NULL,
  `email` varchar(320) DEFAULT NULL,
  `address` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `emergency_contact` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `emergency_phone` varchar(20) DEFAULT NULL,
  `dojo_id` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `coach_id` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `current_belt_level_id` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `status` enum('active','inactive','suspended') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
  `join_date` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `notes` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `password` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `coach` varchar(100) DEFAULT '賴政堡教練',
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `students_student_number_unique` (`student_number`),
  KEY `students_dojo_id_dojos_id_fk` (`dojo_id`),
  KEY `students_coach_id_coaches_id_fk` (`coach_id`),
  KEY `students_current_belt_level_id_belt_levels_id_fk` (`current_belt_level_id`),
  CONSTRAINT `students_coach_id_coaches_id_fk` FOREIGN KEY (`coach_id`) REFERENCES `coaches` (`id`) ON DELETE NO ACTION ON UPDATE NO ACTION,
  CONSTRAINT `students_current_belt_level_id_belt_levels_id_fk` FOREIGN KEY (`current_belt_level_id`) REFERENCES `belt_levels` (`id`) ON DELETE NO ACTION ON UPDATE NO ACTION,
  CONSTRAINT `students_dojo_id_dojos_id_fk` FOREIGN KEY (`dojo_id`) REFERENCES `dojos` (`id`) ON DELETE NO ACTION ON UPDATE NO ACTION
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=191 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

-- Table structure for table `training_schedules`

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `training_schedules`;
/*!40101 SET @saved_cs_client     = @@character_set_client */;
/*!40101 SET character_set_client = utf8mb4 */;
CREATE TABLE `training_schedules` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `training_date` timestamp NOT NULL,
  `venue` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `schedule_day` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `schedule_time` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `status` enum('active','cancelled') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
  `notes` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `updated_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=212 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;
/*!40101 SET character_set_client = @saved_cs_client */;
